Let's check _fft_grad code:
def _fft_grad(weights, a):
    n = a.size; L=weights.size; N=pow2(L+n-1)
    fw = rfft(weights, n=N)
    fa = rfft(a, n=N)
    c = irfft(fw * np.conj(fa), n=N)[:n]
    return 2*c

We need estimate if correct gradient can reach 1.502. Need maybe incorporate "coordinate descent on active coefficients"? Let's investigate mathematical structure and potential exact solutions.

The objective is homogeneous; set S=1. We need minimize max b. This is a continuous nonconvex "minimax" over simplex. Could use "mutex" updates: For a given direction u (sum zero, with only small changes respecting nonneg), score change first order = 2 (g·u). Mirror descent chooses u proportional -a g.

Could use L-BFGS on smooth log-sum-exp. Maybe custom exponentiated gradient with correct gradient and stronger acceptance will do.

Let's refine optimizer design.

Objective smoothing options:
A. Max surrogate: p_k = 1/|A| for active set A (e.g. top K or all b > threshold). Gradient g=2 corr(p,a). This is direct subgradient of max if A active. But if choose top K, may reduce peaks rapidly. Use p uniform over active with small background epsilon to avoid zero.
B. Softmax: p_k ∝ exp(β b_k). With β adapted. Equivalent to log-sum-exp gradient. Good for smoothness.
C. Exponential tilt with focus on gap: p ∝ exp(-γ(maxb-b)/scale). This is same with β=γ/scale and subtract max.

Update:
Given a (>0), p, gradient g. For entropy mirror on simplex, natural gradient step: a_{new} ∝ a * exp(-η g) but this is not exactly mirror descent unless g is gradient of f. For f = ∑ p b (linear in g?), gradient g. Mirror descent in entropy: minimize f(a)+ (1/η)KL(a_new||a) -> a_new ∝ a exp(-η g). Yes.
But g can be large; use normalization. Use G = (1/β) log∑ exp(β b). gradient = (2/β) corr(p_β,a). Step size maybe small.

We can use adaptive step with surrogate: At current a, linear approximation of objective via p: L_new = L_old + g·(a_new-a). Since sum a_new=sum a, constant term. For max, not guaranteed. Use acceptance if new_score <= current_score or surrogate improvement. We can do line search: try several η.

Instead of exponential update that can underflow/overconcentrate, use additive multiplicative weights:
a_new = a + η * (q - a) where q is some target distribution? For minimizing weighted convolution, maybe q_i ∝ 1/(1+η g_i)? Not.

Mirror descent with exponent is standard for nonnegative. Need handle small a. Use log domain: v = log a. Let a = exp(v)/Z. Gradient in v: ∇_v F = g - a (g·a) (since a=∂/∂v log softmax). Use natural gradient descent on v: v_new = v - η (g - mean(a,g)). This is equivalent to multiplicative update. This avoids explicit clamp; can allow v large negative. We can keep v finite by clipping. This is better. We can update v = v - η * h, where h = g - s (s = ∑a_j g_j). Then normalize. If v goes below -745, a underflows to 0. We can clip v to [-700, 700] or rescale (v -= max) each iter. But clipping can distort. Use floor on a if needed.

Step size: For log-sum-exp with β, h scale? We can normalize h by its max absolute or standard deviation to make step dimensionless. Previously gd = grad - dot(a,grad); maxg; gd/=maxg; step eta 0.3. That may be okay. But for log-sum-exp, gradient magnitude includes 1/β, weights sum 1, g ~ 2 a? If b ~1/n, a~1/n, grad ~1/n, normalized. Good.

Need derive correct _fft_grad in log domain? Use weights p sum 1, return 2*corr(p,a). Then h= g - a@g (sum zero in a-weighted sense). Normalize by maxabs. Update v -= eta*h_norm.

But if h is normalized, step eta can be constant ~0.1-1.0. Need line search. Since v updates can be large if h has spikes. We can clip h to e.g. [-3,3] after normalization? Normalization ensures max 1.

Acceptance: Compute b_new, score_new. Also compute weighted objective W = ∑p b (maybe p based old). Since β smoothing maybe. Accept if score_new < best_score or score_new < current_score - tol or W_new < W_old - tol and score not too worse. To avoid local, use exploration.

However pure deterministic gradient from local may converge to local. Need many restarts and random perturbations. We can implement a "multi-start" loop within time: each start initialize (given, pair, random, Fourier), run optimizer for allocated time with restarts inside. Keep global best.

- _make_weights_max(b, focus, eps=1e-6):
   L=b.size; maxb=b.max(); if maxb <=1e-300: return uniform.
   gap=(maxb-b)/maxb  # [0,...]
   with errstate: p=np.exp(-focus*gap)
   s=p.sum(); if s<1e-300: p=zeros; p[np.where(gap==0)]=1; s=...
   p = p/s
   if eps>0: p = (1-eps)*p + eps/L
   return p

- _make_weights_l2(b): return b / b.sum() (b sum ~1; if sum<=0 uniform).

Let's consider whether correct gradient implementation is truly correct for objective with normalized sum and log domain. We use weights p sum1. Objective F=∑ p_k b_k (linear surrogate). gradient g_i=2∑_j a_j p_{i+j}. We compute c=irrfft(fa*conj(fp)). Let's verify with a small example using code mentally. n=2, p=[p0,p1,p2], a=[a0,a1]. N=4? A_k=∑_j a_j e^{-2πijk/N}; P_k^*=∑_l p_l e^{+2πilk/N}. Product sum = ∑_{j,l} a_j p_l e^{2πik(l-j)/N}. IFFT gives ∑_{j,l} a_j p_l δ_{i,l-j mod N} = ∑_j a_j p_{i+j mod N}. For N large and i=0: p0a0+p1a1+p2a2(0)+... =p0a0+p1a1. i=1:p1a0+p2a1. Correct. Times 2 if needed. We normalize, factor irrelevant. Good.

Let's flesh _optimize in detail.

```
def _optimize(a0, time_limit, rng, objective='max', focus_start=10.0, max_focus=2000.0, eta_start=0.08, label='', verbose=False, allow_score_worse=0.002, max_resets=30):
    n=int(a0.size)
    a=np.asarray(a0,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
       a=np.ones(n)/n
       s=1.0
    else:
       a=a/s
    N=_pow2(3*n-2)
    L=2*n-1
    v=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); v=v-v.max()
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=inf; cur_score=inf; b=None; fa=None
    focus=float(focus_start); eta=float(eta_start); eta_min=0.005; eta_max=3.0
    no_improve=0; max_no=300; resets=0; T=1e-4
    t0=time.monotonic(); it=0
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
       if b is None:
          fa=np.fft.rfft(a,n=N); b=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,n=N).real[:L]; b=np.maximum(b,0.0); cur_score=_internal_score(a,b)
       if cur_score < best_score -1e-12:
          best_score=cur_score; best_a=a.copy(); no_improve=0
       else: no_improve +=1
       if verbose and it%2000==0: print(...)
       if no_improve > max_no:
          resets +=1
          if resets > max_resets: break
          no_improve=0; max_no=int(max_no*1.25)
          # generate perturbation
          if resets %3 ==0:
             v = np.log(np.maximum(best_a,1e-300)); v = v - v.max(); v += rng.normal(0,0.15,n); v = v-v.max(); a = np.exp(v); a/=a.sum()
          elif resets%3==1:
             # blend with random positive
             fresh = _random_positive(n, rng) if n<=20000 else None
             if fresh is None: fresh=_anti_log_init(n,rng,3)
             a = 0.75*best_a + 0.25*fresh; a /= a.sum(); v=np.log(max(a,1e-300))-max...
          else:
             v = ... noise 0.3
          b=None; fa=None; cur_score=inf
          focus = float(rng.choice([5,10,20,50,100,300,1000]))
          eta=0.08
          T=max(T*0.5,1e-6)
          continue
       # weights
       if objective == 'l2':
           p = b.copy(); ps=p.sum(); if ps>1e-300: p=p/ps else uniform
           # maybe sharpen p? p = p ** gamma? For L2, gamma >1 emphasizes peaks. Could use gamma = 2+? But p already b. We can use p = (b/b.max())^power? For l2 objective, exact weights b. For surrogate max-soft, use max.
       else:
           p = _make_weights_max(b, focus, active_mix=0.5, eps=1e-6)
       if fa is None: # should not
           fa=np.fft.rfft(a,n=N)
       fp = np.fft.rfft(p,n=N)
       g = np.fft.irfft(fa * np.conj(fp), n=N).real[:n]
       h = g - float(np.dot(a,g))
       maxh = float(np.max(np.abs(h)))
       if maxh < 1e-16: break
       h = h / maxh
       v_new = v - eta*h
       v_new = v_new - v_new.max()
       a_new = np.exp(v_new)
       sn = float(a_new.sum())
       if sn < 1e-15: # underflow? but max exp=1, so no
           break
       a_new /= sn
       fa_new = np.fft.rfft(a_new,n=N)
       b_new = np.fft.irfft(fa_new*fa_new,n=N).real[:L]
       b_new = np.maximum(b_new,0.0)
       score_new = _internal_score(a_new,b_new)
       if not np.isfinite(score_new):
          eta=max(eta*0.7,eta_min); continue
       w_old = float(np.dot(p,b))
       w_new = float(np.dot(p,b_new))
       accept=False
       if score_new < best_score -1e-12: accept=True
       elif score_new < cur_score -1e-12: accept=True
       elif w_new < w_old -1e-12 and score_new < cur_score*(1.0 + allow_score_worse): accept=True
       elif rng.random() < np.exp(-max(0.0, score_new-cur_score)/T) and score_new < best_score*1.002:
           accept=True
       if accept:
           a=a_new; v=v_new; fa=fa_new; b=b_new; cur_score=score_new; eta=min(eta*1.05, eta_max); T=max(T*0.999,1e-6)
       else:
           eta=max(eta*0.75, eta_min)
           if eta < 0.03:
               v = v + rng.normal(0,0.05,n); v=v-v.max(); a=np.exp(v); a/=a.sum(); b=None; fa=None; cur_score=inf
       if objective=='max':
          focus = min(focus*1.02, max_focus)
          T=max(T*0.9999,1e-6)
       it +=1
    # at end, maybe evaluate current if better? best already
    return best_a, float(best_score), a, float(cur_score)
```

Some details:
- In Metropolis exp, if score_new-cur_score large > 50*T, exp underflows to 0; use if delta < 50*T.
- allow_score_worse for L2: maybe 0.02 to allow flatten.
- active_mix 0.5 could make p active uniform over near-max. For max objective with binomial, active set first n, p uniform first n. Good.

If active unique, p_active one-hot; active_mix might make top-1 weight 0.5, soft rest. Good.

- eps background: p = (1-eps)*p_norm + eps/L. Keep small 1e-6.

```
_BINOMIAL_CACHE = {}
def _binomial_init(n, tail_boost=1.0, alpha=None):
    if alpha is None:
       if n in _BINOMIAL_CACHE: q = _BINOMIAL_CACHE[n].copy()
       else:
          q=np.empty(n); q[0]=1.0
          for i in range(1,n): q[i]=q[i-1]*(2*i-1)/(2.0*i)
          _BINOMIAL_CACHE[n]=q.copy()
          q=q.copy()
       if tail_boost !=1 and n>1:
           k=max(1,int(0.1*n)); q[-k:]*=tail_boost
    else:
       idx=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=np.float64); q=idx**(-float(alpha)); q[0]=1.0? This makes q0 maybe? If idx starts1, q0=1. For α.
    s=q.sum(); if s<=0: q=ones/n else q/=s
    return q

Let's refine _make_weights_max:
```
def _make_weights_max(b, focus, active_mix=0.5, active_tol=1e-5, eps=1e-6):
    L=int(b.size); maxb=float(b.max())
    if maxb <= 1e-300:
       return np.full(L,1.0/L)
    gap=(maxb-b)/maxb
    p = np.exp(-focus*gap)
    s=p.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300:
       p = np.zeros(L); p[np.where(gap == 0.0)[0]] = 1.0; s=p.sum()
       if s <=0: p[int(np.argmax(b))]=1; s=1
    p = p/s
    if active_mix > 0:
       active = gap <= active_tol
       cnt = active.sum()
       if cnt > 0 and cnt < L:
           pa = np.zeros(L); pa[active]=1.0/cnt
           p = (1-active_mix)*p + active_mix*pa
       elif cnt == L:
           p = np.full(L,1.0/L) # if all equal
    if eps > 0:
       p = (1-eps)*p + eps/L
    # ensure sum1
    ps=p.sum(); if ps>0: p/=ps
    return p
```

Let's write _optimize in detail with variables not shadowing.

```
def _optimize(a0, time_limit, rng, objective='max', focus_start=20.0, max_focus=3000.0,
              eta_start=0.10, label='', verbose=False, allow_score_worse=0.002,
              max_resets=30, no_improve_start=300):
    n = int(a0.size)
    if n < 2:
       a = np.ones(2)/2; ...
    a = _clean_normalize(a0,n)
    N = _pow2(3*n - 2)
    L = 2*n -1
    v = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    v = v - v.max()
    best_a = a.copy(); best_score = float('inf')
    cur_a = a.copy(); cur_score = float('inf')
    b = None; fa = None
    focus = float(focus_start); eta = float(eta_start)
    eta_min=0.005; eta_max=3.0
    no_improve=0; max_no=no_improve_start; resets=0
    last_improve = float('inf')
    T = 1e-4
    t0=time.monotonic()
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() - t0 < float(time_limit):
       if b is None:
          fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=N)
          b = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, n=N).real[:L]
          b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
          cur_score = _internal_score(a,b)
       if not np.isfinite(cur_score):
          # reset
       cur_improved = False
       if cur_score < best_score - 1e-11:
          best_score = cur_score; best_a = a.copy(); no_improve=0; last_improve=cur_score
       elif cur_score < last_improve - 1e-11:
          last_improve = cur_score; no_improve=0
       else:
          no_improve +=1
       if verbose and it % 5000 ==0: print...
       if no_improve > max_no:
          resets+=1
          if resets > max_resets: break
          no_improve=0; max_no=int(max_no*1.25); last_improve=float('inf')
          # choose perturbation
          base = best_a
          if resets %3 ==0:
             v = np.log(np.maximum(base,1e-300)); v = v - v.max()
             sigma = 0.1 + 0.05*min(resets,5)
             v += rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=n)
          elif resets%3 ==1:
             fresh = _random_positive(n, rng)
             a_blend = 0.7*base + 0.3*fresh; s=a_blend.sum(); if s>0: a_blend/=s
             v = np.log(np.maximum(a_blend,1e-300)); v=v-v.max()
          else:
             fresh = _anti_log_init(n, rng, amp=3.0)
             a_blend = 0.5*base + 0.5*fresh; normalize
             v = ...
          v = v - v.max(); a = np.exp(v); ss=a.sum(); if ss>0: a/=ss
          b=None; fa=None; cur_score=float('inf')
          if objective=='max':
             focus = float(rng.choice([5.0, 10.0, 20.0, 50.0, 100.0, 300.0, 1000.0]))
          eta = float(eta_start)
          T = max(T*0.5, 1e-6)
          it +=1
          continue
       # weights
       if objective == 'l2':
          p = b.copy(); ps = float(p.sum())
          if ps > 1e-300: p /= ps
          else: p = np.full(L, 1.0/L)
          active_mix=0.0
       else:
          active_mix = max(0.0, 0.6 - focus / 200.0)
          p = _make_weights_max(b, focus, active_mix, 1e-5, 1e-6)
       if fa is None:
          fa = np.fft.rfft(a,n=N)
       fp = np.fft.rfft(p, n=N)
       g = np.fft.irfft(fa * np.conj(fp), n=N).real[:n]
       h = g - float(np.dot(a, g))
       maxh = float(np.max(np.abs(h)))
       if maxh < 1e-16:
          break
       h = h / maxh
       v_new = v - eta * h
       v_new = v_new - v_new.max()
       a_new = np.exp(v_new)
       sn = float(a_new.sum())
       if sn <= 1e-300:
          break
       a_new = a_new / sn
       fa_new = np.fft.rfft(a_new, n=N)
       b_new = np.fft.irfft(fa_new * fa_new, n=N).real[:L]
       b_new = np.maximum(b_new, 0.0)
       score_new = _internal_score(a_new, b_new)
       if not np.isfinite(score_new):
          eta = max(eta * 0.7, eta_min)
          it +=1
          continue
       w_old = float(np.dot(p, b))
       w_new = float(np.dot(p, b_new))
       accept = False
       if score_new < best_score - 1e-12:
          accept = True
       elif score_new < cur_score - 1e-12:
          accept = True
       elif w_new < w_old - 1e-12 and score_new < cur_score * (1.0 + float(allow_score_worse)):
          accept = True
       else:
          delta = score_new - cur_score
          if delta >= 0.0 and delta < 60.0 * T:
             prob = float(np.exp(-delta / T))
             if rng.random() < prob and score_new < best_score * (1.0 + float(allow_score_worse)):
                accept = True
       if accept:
          a = a_new; v = v_new; fa = fa_new; b = b_new; cur_score = score_new
          eta = min(eta * 1.05, eta_max)
          T = max(T * 0.999, 1e-6)
       else:
          eta = max(eta * 0.75, eta_min)
          if eta < 0.03:
             # small kick
             v = v + rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, size=n)
             v = v - v.max()
             a = np.exp(v); ss=a.sum(); if ss>0: a/=ss
             b = None; fa = None; cur_score = float('inf')
       if objective == 'max':
          focus = min(focus * 1.02, float(max_focus))
       it +=1
    # ensure best
    if best_score == inf:
       best_score = cur_score if np.isfinite(cur_score) else _internal_score(a,b)
       best_a = a.copy()
    return best_a, float(best_score), a, float(cur_score)
```

Now, code for _anti_log_init:
```
def _anti_log_init(n, rng, amp=3.0):
   n=int(n)
   if n<2: return np.ones(max(1,n))/...
   m=n//2
   z=np.zeros(n)
   v=rng.normal(0.0,1.0,m)
   v = v - v.mean()
   std=v.std()
   if std>1e-12: v = amp*v/std
   z[:m]=v
   if n%2==0:
      z[m:] = -v[::-1]
   else:
      z[m+1:] = -v[::-1]
      z[m]=0.0
   z -= z.max()
   a=np.exp(z)
   a = np.maximum(a, 1e-300)
   s=a.sum()
   if s>0: a/=s
   return a
```
If m=0? n=1.

_random_positive:
```
def _random_positive(n, rng):
   n=int(n)
   z=rng.normal(0,1,n)
   z -= z.max()
   a=np.exp(z)
   a=np.maximum(a,1e-300)
   s=a.sum(); if s>0: a/=s
   return a
```

_pair_logistic_init maybe:
```
def _pair_logistic_init(n, rng):
   n=int(n); m=n//2
   if m<1: return np.ones(n)/n
   z = rng.normal(0,1,m)
   x = 1.0/(1.0+np.exp(-z))
   x = np.clip(x, 1e-8, 1.0-1e-8)
   a=np.empty(n)
   a[:m]=x/m
   a[m:]=(1.0 - x[::-1])/m
   # if n odd, middle? If n=2m+1, a[m] unassigned. Set 1/m? But sum pairs m*1/m=1, middle would extra. For odd, omit or set small. We can only use even n. For safety, if n%2: a[m]=0. Then sum=1? pairs sum=1. Good.
   if n%2==1: a[m]=0.0
   s=a.sum(); if s>0: a/=s (should be1)
   return a
```
We may not use.

Now, _fast_score:
```
def _fast_score(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   n=a.size
   if n==0: return inf
   a=np.nan_to_num...
   s=a.sum(); if s<... return inf
   N=_pow2(max(3*n-2,1))
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,n=N)
   b=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,n=N).real[:2*n-1]
   b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
   return _internal_score(a,b)
```
It doesn't normalize; score invariant. Use for given.

Now, _update_best:
```
def _update_best(best_a, best_score, a, score):
   if a is None or score is None: return best_a,best_score
   try: score=float(score)
   except: return
   if not np.isfinite(score): return
   if a is not None:
      a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
      if a.size>0:
         s=a.sum()
         if s>0.01? no, for internal can be any; but if sum small? We normalized.
         if score < best_score:
            return a.copy(), score
   return best_a,best_score
```

def _pow2(x):

If g_i > W, h_i positive, a_i decreased. Does that reduce W? Linear change dW = g·da. da_i ≈ a_i(-eta h_i). Weighted sum a_i h_i = a_i g_i - W a_i; ∑ a_i h_i=0. dW ≈ -eta ∑ a_i g_i^2 ≤0 plus? ```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / (sum(a))**2.

The key ingredients are:
  * an exact FFT-based gradient for the self-convolution objective,
  * a strong constructive initialization from the truncated binomial series
    q_k = binom(2k,k) / 4^k, whose convolution is flat on the first n terms,
  * mirror descent on the simplex (multiplicative / log-domain updates),
  * several objective modes: direct max-peak soft gradients and L2 flatness,
  * adaptive step size, focus, random resets, and multi-start refinement.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

_BINOMIAL_CACHE = {}


def _pow2(x):
    x = int(x)
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def _clean_normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def _internal_score(a, b):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or n == 0 or b.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    maxb = float(b.max())
    if maxb <= 0.0:
        return 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * maxb / (s * s))


def _fast_score(a):
    a = _clean_normalize(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return float("inf")
    N = _pow2(max(1, 3 * n - 2))
    L = 2 * n - 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=N)
    b = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, n=N).real[:L]
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    return _internal_score(a, b)


def _random_positive(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    z = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=n)
    z -= z.max()
    a = np.exp(z)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-300)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return a / s


def _anti_log_init(n, rng, amp=3.0):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    m = n // 2
    if m <= 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    v = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=m)
    v = v - float(v.mean())
    std = float(v.std())
    if std > 1e-12:
        v = float(amp) * v / std
    z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    z[:m] = v
    if n % 2 == 0:
        z[m:] = -v[::-1]
    else:
        z[m + 1:] = -v[::-1]
        z[m] = 0.0
    z -= z.max()
    a = np.exp(z)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-300)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return a / s


def _pair_logistic_init(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    m = n // 2
    if m <= 0:
        return np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    z = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=m)
    x = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-z))
    x = np.clip(x, 1e-8, 1.0 - 1e-8)
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[:m] = x / float(m)
    a[m:] = (1.0 - x[::-1]) / float(m)
    if n % 2 == 1:
        a[m] = 0.0
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return a / s


def _binomial_init(n, tail_boost=1.0, alpha=None):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64)

    q = None
    if alpha is None:
        cached = _BINOMIAL_CACHE.get(n)
        if cached is not None:
            q = cached.copy()
        else:
            q = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
            q[0] = 1.0
            for i in range(1, n):
                q[i] = q[i - 1] * (2.0 * i - 1.0) / (2.0 * i)
            _BINOMIAL_CACHE[n] = q.copy()
            q = q.copy()
    else:
        idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        q = idx ** (-float(alpha))

    if tail_boost != 1.0 and n > 8:
        k = max(1, int(0.10 * n))
        q[-k:] *= float(tail_boost)

    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64)
    q = np.nan_to_num(q, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    s = float(q.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return q / s


def _make_weights_max(b, focus, active_mix=0.5, active_tol=1e-5, eps=1e-6):
    L = int(b.size)
    if L <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    maxb = float(b.max())
    if maxb <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64)

    gap = (maxb - b) / maxb

    with np.errstate(over="ignore", under="ignore"):
        p = np.exp(-float(focus) * gap)

    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        idx = int(np.argmax(b))
        p[idx] = 1.0
        s = 1.0
    else:
        p = p / s

    active_mix = float(active_mix)
    if active_mix > 0.0:
        active = gap <= float(active_tol)
        cnt = int(active.sum())
        if 0 < cnt < L:
            pa = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            pa[active] = 1.0 / float(cnt)
            p = (1.0 - active_mix) * p + active_mix * pa
        elif cnt == L:
            p = np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64)

    if eps > 0.0:
        p = (1.0 - eps) * p + eps / float(L)
        ps = float(p.sum())
        if ps > 1e-300:
            p /= ps
    return p


def _make_weights_l2(b):
    L = int(b.size)
    if L <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64)
    return b / s


def _update_best(best_a, best_score, a, score):
    if a is None or score is None:
        return best_a, best_score
    try:
        score = float(score)
    except Exception:
        return best_a, best_score
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return best_a, best_score
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return best_a, best_score
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return best_a, best_score
    if score < best_score:
        return a.copy(), score
    return best_a, best_score


def _optimize(a0, time_limit, rng, objective="max", focus_start=20.0,
              max_focus=3000.0, eta_start=0.10, label="", verbose=False,
              allow_score_worse=0.002, max_resets=30, no_improve_start=400):
    """
    Mirror-descent optimizer.

    objective = 'max':
        soft gradient of max(conv) using high-conv coefficients.
    objective = 'l2':
        smooth flatness objective sum(conv^2).

    Returns:
        best_a, best_score, current_a, current_score
    """
    a = _clean_normalize(a0)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 1:
        return a, 0.0, a, 0.0

    time_limit = max(5.0, float(time_limit))
    N = _pow2(max(1, 3 * n - 2))
    L = 2 * n - 1

    v = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    v = v - v.max()
    v = np.maximum(v, -50.0)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = float("inf")
    cur_score = float("inf")
    last_improve = float("inf")

    b = None
    fa = None

    focus = float(focus_start)
    eta = float(eta_start)
    eta_min = 0.005
    eta_max = 3.0

    no_improve = 0
    max_no = int(max(50, no_improve_start))
    resets = 0
    T = 1e-4

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        if b is None:
            fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=N)
            b = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, n=N).real[:L]
            b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
            cur_score = _internal_score(a, b)

        if not np.isfinite(cur_score):
            resets += 1
            if resets > max_resets:
                break
            v = np.log(np.maximum(best_a, 1e-300))
            v = v - v.max()
            v += rng.normal(0.0, 0.2, size=n)
            v = v - v.max()
            v = np.maximum(v, -50.0)
            a = np.exp(v)
            ss = float(a.sum())
            if ss > 1e-300:
                a /= ss
            else:
                a = _clean_normalize(a)
            b = None
            fa = None
            cur_score = float("inf")
            eta = float(eta_start)
            focus = float(rng.choice([5.0, 10.0, 20.0, 50.0, 100.0, 300.0]))
            it += 1
            continue

        if cur_score < best_score - 1e-11:
            best_score = cur_score
            best_a = a.copy()
            no_improve = 0
            last_improve = cur_score
        elif cur_score < last_improve - 1e-11:
            last_improve = cur_score
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if verbose and (it % 5000 == 0):
            print(
                f"[{label}] it={it} t={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s "
                f"score={cur_score:.8f} best={best_score:.8f} "
                f"focus={focus:.1f} eta={eta:.4f} resets={resets}"
            )

        if no_improve > max_no:
            resets += 1
            if resets > max_resets:
                break
            no_improve = 0
            max_no = int(max_no * 1.25)
            last_improve = float("inf")

            base = best_a
            if resets % 3 == 0:
                vv = np.log(np.maximum(base, 1e-300))
                vv = vv - vv.max()
                sigma = 0.10 + 0.04 * min(resets, 6)
                vv += rng.normal(0.0, float(sigma), size=n)
                v = vv
            elif resets % 3 == 1:
                fresh = _random_positive(n, rng)
                blend = 0.72 * base + 0.28 * fresh
                ss = float(blend.sum())
                if ss > 1e-300:
                    blend /= ss
                else:
                    blend = _clean_normalize(blend)
                v = np.log(np.maximum(blend, 1e-300))
            else:
                fresh = _anti_log_init(n, rng, amp=3.5)
                blend = 0.55 * base + 0.45 * fresh
                ss = float(blend.sum())
                if ss > 1e-300:
                    blend /= ss
                else:
                    blend = _clean_normalize(blend)
                v = np.log(np.maximum(blend, 1e-300))

            v = v - v.max()
            v = np.maximum(v, -50.0)
            a = np.exp(v)
            ss = float(a.sum())
            if ss > 1e-300:
                a /= ss
            else:
                a = _clean_normalize(a)
            b = None
            fa = None
            cur_score = float("inf")
            if objective == "max":
                focus = float(rng.choice([5.0, 10.0, 20.0, 50.0, 100.0, 300.0, 1000.0]))
            eta = float(eta_start)
            T = max(T * 0.5, 1e-6)
            it += 1
            continue

        if objective == "l2":
            p = _make_weights_l2(b)
            active_mix = 0.0
        else:
            active_mix = max(0.0, 0.60 - focus / 200.0)
            p = _make_weights_max(b, focus, active_mix, 1e-5, 1e-6)

        if fa is None:
            fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=N)

        fp = np.fft.rfft(p, n=N)
        g = np.fft.irfft(fa * np.conj(fp), n=N).real[:n]

        # Natural gradient under sum(a)=1.
        h = g - float(np.dot(a, g))
        maxh = float(np.max(np.abs(h)))
        if maxh < 1e-16:
            break
        h = h / maxh

        v_new = v - eta * h
        v_new = v_new - v_new.max()
        v_new = np.maximum(v_new, -50.0)

        a_new = np.exp(v_new)
        sn = float(a_new.sum())
        if sn <= 1e-300:
            break
        a_new /= sn

        fa_new = np.fft.rfft(a_new, n=N)
        b_new = np.fft.irfft(fa_new * fa_new, n=N).real[:L]
        b_new = np.maximum(b_new, 0.0)
        score_new = _internal_score(a_new, b_new)

        if not np.isfinite(score_new):
            eta = max(eta * 0.7, eta_min)
            it += 1
            continue

        w_old = float(np.dot(p, b))
        w_new = float(np.dot(p, b_new))

        accept = False
        if score_new < best_score - 1e-12:
            accept = True
        elif score_new < cur_score - 1e-12:
            accept = True
        elif w_new < w_old - 1e-12 and score_new < cur_score * (1.0 + float(allow_score_worse)):
            accept = True
        else:
            delta = score_new - cur_score
            if 0.0 <= delta < 60.0 * T:
                prob = float(np.exp(-delta / T))
                if rng.random() < prob and score_new < best_score * (1.0 + float(allow_score_worse)):
                    accept = True

        if accept:
            a = a_new
            v = v_new
            fa = fa_new
            b = b_new
            cur_score = score_new
            eta = min(eta * 1.03, eta_max)
            T = max(T * 0.999, 1e-6)
        else:
            eta = max(eta * 0.75, eta_min)
            if eta < 0.03:
                v = v + rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, size=n)
                v = v - v.max()
                v = np.maximum(v, -50.0)
                a = np.exp(v)
                ss = float(a.sum())
                if ss > 1e-300:
                    a /= ss
                else:
                    a = _clean_normalize(a)
                b = None
                fa = None
                cur_score = float("inf")

        if objective == "max":
            focus = min(focus * 1.02, float(max_focus))

        it += 1

    if not np.isfinite(cur_score):
        try:
            cur_score = _fast_score(a)
        except Exception:
            cur_score = float("inf")

    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_score = cur_score if np.isfinite(cur_score) else float("inf")
        best_a = a.copy()

    return best_a, float(best_score), a, float(cur_score)


def _safe_evaluate(seq):
    ev = globals().get("evaluate_sequence")
    if ev is None or not callable(ev):
        return float("inf")
    try:
        return float(ev(seq))
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    seed = int(seed)

    # Use the full public runtime when possible.
    provided = float(budget_s)
    if provided > 1000.0:
        total = min(2400.0, provided)
    elif provided > 100.0:
        total = 2370.0
    else:
        total = max(60.0, provided)

    now0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = now0 + total - 40.0

    print("starting improved non-negative convolution optimizer")

    best_a = None
    best_score = float("inf")
    task_rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # Optional seed from previous state of the art.
    try:
        given = globals().get("height_sequence_1")
        if given is not None:
            g = np.asarray(given, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
            g = np.clip(g, 0.0, 1000.0)
            gs = float(g.sum())
            if g.size >= 8 and g.size <= 12000 and gs > 0.01:
                gn = g / gs
                sc = _fast_score(gn)
                best_a, best_score = _update_best(best_a, best_score, gn, sc)
                print(f"given sequence: n={gn.size} internal_score={sc:.8f}")
    except Exception as exc:
        print("given sequence check failed:", repr(exc))

    # Refine the previous incumbent if present.
    if best_a is not None and best_a.size <= 10000:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 230.0:
            alloc = min(180.0, rem - 40.0)
            if alloc > 60.0:
                print(f"refining previous incumbent, alloc={alloc:.1f}s")
                a1, s1, c1, cs1 = _optimize(
                    best_a.copy(), alloc, task_rng,
                    objective="max",
                    focus_start=60.0,
                    max_focus=3000.0,
                    eta_start=0.05,
                    allow_score_worse=0.001,
                    label="prev"
                )
                best_a, best_score = _update_best(best_a, best_score, a1, s1)
                best_a, best_score = _update_best(best_a, best_score, c1, cs1)
                print(f"after prev refine: best={best_score:.8f}")

    # Main search at n=8192, which is safely fast for final direct convolution.
    n_main = 8192
    a_bin = _binomial_init(n_main)

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 500.0:
        alloc = min(1000.0, max(120.0, (rem) * 0.45))
        print(f"main binomial max phase: alloc={alloc:.1f}s best={best_score:.8f}")
        a1, s1, c1, cs1 = _optimize(
            a_bin.copy(), alloc, task_rng,
            objective="max",
            focus_start=20.0,
            max_focus=4000.0,
            eta_start=0.10,
            allow_score_worse=0.002,
            label="bin-main",
            verbose=True
        )
        best_a, best_score = _update_best(best_a, best_score, a1, s1)
        best_a, best_score = _update_best(best_a, best_score, c1, cs1)
        print(f"after main binomial: best={best_score:.8f}")

    # L2 flatness phase from the binomial seed, then max-peak polish.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 550.0:
        t_l2 = min(240.0, max(80.0, rem * 0.15))
        print(f"binomial L2 phase: alloc={t_l2:.1f}s")
        a_l2, s_l2, c_l2, cs_l2 = _optimize(
            _binomial_init(n_main).copy(), t_l2, task_rng,
            objective="l2",
            eta_start=0.20,
            allow_score_worse=0.05,
            label="bin-l2"
        )
        best_a, best_score = _update_best(best_a, best_score, a_l2, s_l2)
        best_a, best_score = _update_best(best_a, best_score, c_l2, cs_l2)

        if np.isfinite(cs_l2) and cs_l2 < s_l2 + 0.02:
            init2 = c_l2
        else:
            init2 = a_l2

        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        t_m2 = min(300.0, rem - 40.0)
        if t_m2 > 80.0:
            print(f"L2-initialized max polish: alloc={t_m2:.1f}s")
            a3, s3, c3, cs3 = _optimize(
                init2.copy(), t_m2, task_rng,
                objective="max",
                focus_start=60.0,
                max_focus=5000.0,
                eta_start=0.08,
                allow_score_worse=0.002,
                label="l2-max"
            )
            best_a, best_score = _update_best(best_a, best_score, a3, s3)
            best_a, best_score = _update_best(best_a, best_score, c3, cs3)
            print(f"after L2 polish: best={best_score:.8f}")

    # Tail-boosted binomial diversity.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 280.0:
        alloc = min(200.0, rem - 40.0)
        a_tb = _binomial_init(n_main, tail_boost=2.0)
        print(f"tail-boost binomial: alloc={alloc:.1f}s")
        a4, s4, c4, cs4 = _optimize(
            a_tb.copy(), alloc, task_rng,
            objective="max",
            focus_start=15.0,
            max_focus=3000.0,
            eta_start=0.08,
            allow_score_worse=0.002,
            label="tail2x"
        )
        best_a, best_score = _update_best(best_a, best_score, a4, s4)
        best_a, best_score = _update_best(best_a, best_score, c4, cs4)
        print(f"after tail boost: best={best_score:.8f}")

    # Anti-log / random diversity.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 220.0:
        alloc = min(150.0, rem - 40.0)
        a_div = _anti_log_init(n_main, task_rng, amp=4.0)
        a_mix = 0.35 * _binomial_init(n_main) + 0.65 * a_div
        sm = float(a_mix.sum())
        if sm > 1e-300:
            a_mix /= sm
        else:
            a_mix = _clean_normalize(a_mix)
        print(f"anti/random diversity: alloc={alloc:.1f}s")
        a5, s5, c5, cs5 = _optimize(
            a_mix.copy(), alloc, task_rng,
            objective="max",
            focus_start=8.0,
            max_focus=2500.0,
            eta_start=0.07,
            allow_score_worse=0.003,
            label="anti"
        )
        best_a, best_score = _update_best(best_a, best_score, a5, s5)
        best_a, best_score = _update_best(best_a, best_score, c5, cs5)
        print(f"after diversity: best={best_score:.8f}")

    # Final intensive refinement of the global best.
    if best_a is None or not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_a = _binomial_init(4096)
        best_score = _fast_score(best_a)

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 70.0:
        alloc = rem - 30.0
        print(f"final refinement: alloc={alloc:.1f}s best={best_score:.8f}")
        af, sf, cf, csf = _optimize(
            best_a.copy(), alloc, task_rng,
            objective="max",
            focus_start=250.0,
            max_focus=12000.0,
            eta_start=0.05,
            allow_score_worse=0.0005,
            label="final",
            verbose=True
        )
        best_a, best_score = _update_best(best_a, best_score, af, sf)
        best_a, best_score = _update_best(best_a, best_score, cf, csf)
        print(f"after final: best={best_score:.8f}")

    # Fallback if anything strange happened.
    if best_a is None or not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_a = _binomial_init(4096)
        best_score = _fast_score(best_a)

    best_a = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    best_a = np.nan_to_num(best_a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    best_a = np.clip(best_a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(best_a.sum())
    if s <= 0.01:
        best_a = _binomial_init(4096)
        s = float(best_a.sum())
    else:
        best_a /= s

    final_seq = [float(x) for x in best_a]

    # Optional exact check for reasonably sized final answer.
    if best_a.size <= 8200 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0:
        exact = _safe_evaluate(final_seq)
        if np.isfinite(exact):
            print(f"final internal={best_score:.8f} exact={exact:.8f} n={best_a.size}")
        else:
            print(f"final internal={best_score:.8f} exact unavailable n={best_a.size}")
    else:
        print(f"final internal={best_score:.8f} n={best_a.size}")

    return final_seq
```
